       A.1. Introduction

        The AZADEGAN Oilfield is Located in Khuzestan Province and is Approximately Located 80

          Kilometers West of Ahwaz City, Near to Iran-Iraq Border. It is in The Northwest of The

      YADAVARAN Field (Fig A.1). The Average Monthly Temperature is 45ºc in summer, and

          Highest Temperature is 52ºc. The Lowest Temperature is -4.4ºc in winter. The water for the

        work in the south of the AZADEGAN will be supplied by tank truck from the water designated

          channel.

         According to the drilling history, the Southern AZADEGAN Oilfield is distributed into three

          blocks as per the geological features with regard to  drilling operation (Fig A.2). This

           distribution may differ according to knowledge accumulated in the future.

               Block I is located in the north part of the Southern AZADEGAN Oilfield in north of

                    drilled well AZA-10. In this block, Gachsaran formation is characterized with abnormal

                high pressure.

               Block II is located between drilled well AZN-10 and AZN-11. In this block, Gachsaran

                 formation is characterized with pressure tending to normal.

               Block III is located to the south of line drilled well AZN-11. In this block, Gachsaran formation

                        is characterized with normal pressure and all drilled wells in this block suffered from complete

                     loss at Pabdeh formation.

        The plan is to drill total 5 appraisal wells with vertical profiles on 5 well pads and all will be

          of producer wells. Totally, 6 wells had been assigned to be drilled throughout the South

         Azadegan oil field which Appraisal well No.1 (AZNS-030) was drilled to total depth of 3055m

     MD in Pad-27 in Sarvak formation and completed on 23 July 2013. The 5 remaining Appraisal

          wells should be drilled. These 5 wells are categorized to 1 Gadvan well and 4 Fahliyan wells.

        APP-04 Well  is the appraisal well in south Azadegan oilfield, Iran. This well shall be

          considered to drill to the Fahliyan formation. Plan is to drill the depth of ±4300 mt to 5” liner

           setting depth. Then 5” liner will be run and cemented. Tarbur, Sarvak, Kazhdumi, Gadvan and

          Fahliyan formation will be perforated and tested by FBDST and finally complete well and

          released the rig.

       A.3. Well Objectives

        APP-04 well is located on pad 44 (Coordinate (UTM): 194483, 3454673, Zone: 39). This well is

          the appraisal well in south Azadegan oilfield, Iran, and shall be considered to drill vertically to

          the Fahliyan formation. Plan is to drill the depth of ±4300 mt to 5” liner setting depth. Then 5”

            liner will be run and cemented. Tarbur, Sarvak, Kazhdumi, Gadvan and Fahliyan formation

           will be perforated and tested by FBDST and Collect PVT Sample from Reservoirs Fluid, then

         complete the well.

              It is planned to take core from the specific intervals in Tarbur, Ilam, Sarvak and Kazhdumi

          Formations.

          Petrophysical Logs (Full Suite, MDT and DSI) shall be taken through reservoir intervals.

       VSP log shall be run throughout this well from target depth to surface to get more data and

          update velocity and structural model.

          Default production formation is Fahliyan formation. But after perform all mentioned tests and

           interpret mentioned logs, production formation will be determined.

          Fig A.3 shows the location of APP-04 on Sarvak top map.

       A.4. Pore and Fracture Pressure Prediction

        As above mentioned  (section A.1.  Introduction)  the Formation Pressure of Gachsaran

         Formation is Different between North Area and South. This Oilfield is divided into three Areas:

               Area I (North of AZN-10): Area I located in north of Y-Coordinate: 3452051, in this

                   area, Gachsaran Formation is characterized with abnormal high pressure.

               Area II (Between AZN-10 and AZN-11): Area  II located between Y-coordinate:

               3452051 and Y-Coordinate: 3443549, in this area, the pressure of Gachsaran formation

                 decreases greatly.

               Area III (South of AZN-11): Area III located the south of Y-Coordinate: 3443549, in

                    this area, the pressure of Gachsaran formation decreases greatly and all existing wells

                 occurred complete loss at Pabdeh formation.

        The area division shall be adjusted according to further actual drilling operation, and quantity

          of wells in different areas could be also adjusted accordingly.

        APP-04 well is located in area I and Gachsaran formation has abnormal pore pressure. Fig A.4

         shows the pore pressure and fracture gradient of area I. The actual formation pressure will be

          determined during drilling operation of APP-04 well.

       A.5. Program Outline

              It is planned to drill and complete a Fahliyan Vertical Producer well to evaluate the Tarbur,

          Sarvak, Kazhdumi, Gadvan and Fahliyan formation and finally complete the well as producing

           well.

            A.5.1. 26” Hole Section

             This section will be drilled ±151 mt through Aghajari formation. Aghajari formation

               consists of a marl, Anhydride sand-shale. For drilling this section 65-68 pcf Bentonite

            mud/Hi-Vis pill gel will be used for hole cleaning. Also Hi-Vis pill will be used to clean

              the hole and since bentonite has the best cake around the well bore, it will make a good

             cake for hole stability. According to previous wells in this field, this section is almost

            problem free. The 20" casing will be set at ±150 mt and it will be cemented in one stage

             with stinger (S.W.: 115 pcf, Accurate CMT program will be issued in due time). N/Up 20-

              3/4" 3K C.H.H. & 20-3/4" 3K annular bop and double rams.

            A.5.2. 17-1/2” Hole Section

           The 17-1/2” hole section will be drilled from ±151 mt to ±1256 mt through Aghajari

             formation and 55 mt inside Gachsaran formation. According to previous wells in this field,

               in this section there is a possibility of complete loss with No Return mud. Due to the Claystone

             and marl characteristic of the formation, bit balling is one of the main problems. Also tight hole

             might be experienced in this interval, so good inhibition will be helpful to provide stable hole

               condition. Drill this section with 67-79 pcf Saturated KCL Polymer Mud (CLAY-BOND

            mud). Run CDR/GR/CALIPER and Sonic log. Run 13-3/8" casing to ±1255 mt and its shoe

               will be set at this depth to case off full soft unconsolidated formation of lower Aghajari and

                 drill the next hole section with higher mud density. It will be cemented in one stage (Lead:

             S.W.: 95 pcf & Tail: 118 pcf - Accurate CMT program will be issued in due time). N/Up

             C.H.S. 20-3/4" 3k x 13-5/8” 5k. N/Up 13-5/8" 10k BOP stack (13-5/8" 5k annular preventer

          + 13-5/8" 10k double rams with shear rams + 13-5/8" 10k single rams).

          July 2016                       Page 10 of 223             Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                 General Information                      Contract No.: MTN-94-854

            A.5.3. 12-1/4” Hole Section

             This Section will be drilled from ±1256 to ±1555 mt through Gachsaran. Gachsaran

             Contain Anhydrite, Limestone, Dolomite, Marl with High Pressure Salt Aquifer, Salt Bed

            and Salt Water. For Drilling this Section the 90-105 pcf Salt Saturated Heavy Weight

            Polymer Mud should be used. The Risks of Drilling this Section is Gachsaran formation

             has the potential of salt water flow. In the upper part of Gachsaran a weak zone has been

              experienced. Layers interbedded in the salts in Gachsaran Members 2 & 3 can have normal

               pressure, and therefore a reduced fracture gradient compared to the salt layers therefore

           Heavy losses might be experienced in Gachsaran. Also Tight hole/stuck pipe in this hole

              section is typically isolated to the Shale/Clay stones and salt beds. CDR/GR/Caliper and

           DSI will be run in open hole. 9-5/8" casing shoe will be set at ±1554 mt at the bottom of

            Gachsaran (5 mt above Asmari formation) to isolate Gachsaran high pressure salt water

              formation. It will be cemented in one stage (Lead: S.W.: 115 pcf & Tail: 118 pcf. Accurate

        CMT program will be issued in due time). Due to existence of salt beds at this section salt

              saturated water will be used for cement job. N/Up T.H.S. 13-5/8” 5k x 11" 10k. N/Up 13-

             5/8” 10k BOP stack. (13-5/8" 5k annular preventer + 13-5/8" 10k double rams with shear

            rams + 13-5/8" 10k single rams). CBL/VDL/CCL/GR will be run to evaluate the cement

               quality behind casing.

            A.5.4. 8-1/2” Hole Section

           The 8-1/2” hole section will be drilled from ±1555 to ±3958 mt through Asmari, Pabdeh,

             Gurpi, Ilam, Laffan, Sarvak, Kazhdumi, Dariyan and Gadvan formations which consist of

             Sandstone, Limestone, Marl and Claystone Contains H2S, CO2, Hydrocarbon and salt

              water. The risks of drilling this section is heavy drilling fluid loss (especially at Pabdeh

              formation), tight hole and stuck pipe based on offset wells. Also there is possibility for

               existence of H2S and CO2 Gases in the reservoir zones (Sarvak, Kazhdumi and Gadvan). 75-

            84 pcf KCl –Polymer Mud (ULTRA-INHIBIT) mud will be used for drilling this section.

            Core will be taken through Tarbur, Ilam, Sarvak and Kazhdumi formation as per program.

            CDR/GR/Caliper, Full Suite, MDT and DSI will be run in open hole. Then 7” liner will be

             run to ±3957 mt (25 mt above Top of Fahliyan, Liner Lap @ ±1484 mt) and cemented in

            one stage (lead: S.W.: 105 pcf & Tail: 115 pcf. Accurate CMT program will be issued in

            due time). SBT-RBT /CCL/GR will be run to evaluate the cement quality behind casing.

          July 2016                       Page 11 of 223             Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                 General Information                      Contract No.: MTN-94-854

            A.5.5. 5-7/8” Hole Section

             A.5.5.1. Main Section

           The 5-7/8” hole section will be drilled from ±3958 to ±4300 mt through Fahliyan

             formations which consist of Limestone, Marl and Claystone Contains Hydrocarbon. The

               risks of drilling this section is mud loss, tight hole and stuck pipe based on offset wells.

            Also there is possibility for existence of H2S and CO2 Gases. 95-110 pcf KCl-Polymer Mud

          (SHALE-DRILL mud) will be used for drilling this section. CDR/ Full Suite, MDT, DSI

               will be run in open hole. Then 5” liner will be run to ±4299 mt (Liner Lap @ ±3887 mt)

            and cemented in one stage (S.W.: 115 pcf. Accurate CMT program will be issued in due

               time). SBT-RBT/CCL/GR will be run to evaluate the cement quality behind casing. Finally

         VSP Log (Zero Offset) will be run from TD to Surface.

             A.5.5.2. Testing Section

              Perforate and perform FBDST at Tarbur, Sarvak (S3-6), Kazhdumi, Gadvan (G5) and

             Fahliyan (F1, F4, F5) by TCP-APR assembly from BTM to upper interval (Tarbur). Using

         CTU to stimulate the well  if required. After finishing FBDST of each interval all

              perforations will be isolated by bridge plug and CMT (if possible) and then perforate and

                 test next interval.

             A.5.5.3. Final Section

                Finally, after finishing FBDST of all intervals, all CMT plugs and bridge plugs will be drill

              out and clean the hole. 5” tieback will be run to previous 5” liner tieback sleeve (±3887 mt)

             and tieback liner to 50 mt above Tarbur perforation. Run completion string as per program

             (completion program will be issued in due time). N/Up well head and X-mass tree. Secure

               the well and release the rig.

       B.1. Formation Description

      A brief description of the stratigraphic units expected to be drilled in South Azadegan typical

          well has been presented in the following breakdown.

        Aghajari Formation

        From Surface to 1201 m MD,        Thickness: 1192 m.

          Aghajari Formation exposes in the surface, this formation overlays continuously Gachsaran

          formation. Aghajari lithology consists of sandstone, siltstone, marl and clay stone layers so that

                it comprises of reddish marls interbedded with sandstone and siltstone layers.

        Gachsaran Formation

        From 1201 to 1560 m MD,          Thickness: 359 m.

          This formation is composing of anhydrite interbedded with gray and red marl, salt beds of

           variable thickness and some thin limestone beds.

         The Gachsaran Formation with a thickness ranges from 400 m to 600 m, thicken towards the

           north. Deposition of the Gachsaran Formation is cyclic. The cycle began with a deposition of

          shallow-water marine carbonates, and then it was overlain by a thick evaporate phase. The

          evaporates were capped by red to variegated marl, which represented the end of cycle.

        Asmari Formation

        From 1560 to 1870 m MD,          Thickness: 310 m.

         The Asmari Formation in Azadegan Field developed a massive sandstone interval, with a few

           thin argillaceous layers in the upper sections. Sands are clear, fine to medium-sorted, and

          poorly consolidated. On the other hand, the lower sections consist of intercalation of sandy,

          chalky, slightly dolomitic high-energy limestone with sands layers.

        March 2016                      Page 42 of 223           Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                 Geological Information                    Contract No.: MTN-94-854

        Pabdeh Formation

        From 1870 to 2253 m MD,          Thickness: 383 m.

         Pabdeh Formation is made of grey to dark-brown argillaceous limestone and grey marl, partly

          Claystone at the top and gradually argillaceous lime stones with marl, grey, buff, brown, soft,

           sticky with some nodular Chert increase at the base.

        Gurpi Formation

        From 2253 to 2555 m MD,          Thickness: 302 m.

          This is formation consists of three main units:

           a) Upper Gurpi Member

          b) Tarbur member

           c) Lower Gurpi Member

                  Upper Gurpi Member

        From 2253 to 2368 m MD,          Thickness: 115 m.

          This Member begins with light gray, gray, off white, soft to medium, sticky argillaceous

          limestone. The Upper Gurpi Member is mostly consisting of gray buff to gray with partly buff

           gray, dark gray, medium hard to hard argillaceous.

                  Tarbur member

        From 2368 to 2443 m MD,          Thickness: 75 m.

         Tarbur Member consisting of cream to light gray, light brown, buff, medium hard, II, A

         Limestone interbedded with chalky limestone and marl beds.

                  Lower Gurpi Member

        From 2443 to 2555 m MD,          Thickness: 112 m.

        The Member consisting of dark gray to gray, light gray, soft to medium hard Argillaceous

          limestone and light cream and soft. II, A. Chalky Limestone beds.

        March 2016                      Page 43 of 223           Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                 Geological Information                    Contract No.: MTN-94-854

        Ilam Formation

        From 2555 to 2670 m MD,          Thickness: 115 m.

          This formation begin with off white, cream, soft to medium hard, pure lime stone and continue

          with marl grey, soft, sticky, red to reddish brown, clay stone, interbedded with Argillaceous

         grey limestone, at the base of this formation appear pure lime stone, white to whitish grey,

        medium hard to hard, some chalky with oil stained.

        Laffan Formation

        From 2670 to 2677 m MD,          Thickness: 7 m.

          This formation is mostly consisting of shale interbedded with clay stone beds. The Shale green

         gray to light green gray, mostly brown gray medium hard, pyritic, medium hard, slightly

          calcareous.

        Sarvak Formation

        From 2677 to 3344 m MD,          Thickness: 667 m.

          This formation is mostly consisting of off white, cream, buff to cream and white to cream, soft,

            soft to medium hard, chalky limestone interbedded with argillaceous limestone.

        Kazhdumi Formation

        From 3344 to 3518 m MD,          Thickness: 174 m.

          In the South Azadegan Field, the upper part of the Kazhdumi Formation is made up of dark-

         grey to dark-brown Shale and marl, dark grey, the middle part developed light-grey and micritic

           to chalky limestone. The lower part is clean channel sands interbedded with argillaceous sands

          with streaks of shale and some anhydrite. This part is equivalent with Azadegan member.

        March 2016                      Page 44 of 223           Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                 Geological Information                    Contract No.: MTN-94-854

        Dariyan Formation

        From 3518 to 3718 m MD,          Thickness: 200 m.

          This formation is started with grey to light grey, some cream limestone, which mostly are

           argillaceous. These layers are followed with off-white to cream, mostly argillaceous limestone.

        Some pyrite is probably occurred in this formation.

        Gadvan Formation

        From 3718 to 3983 m MD,                 Thickness: 265 m.

        The Gadvan formation divided to three part.

                  The upper part appear with occurrence of terrigenous rock. The lithology is

                 composed og grey Claystone and some shale.

                  The Khalij member appear with pure limestone, cream, light brown and in some

                       intervals oil stain.

                  The lower part consisting of alternating of marl, argillaceous limestone, limestone,

                     Claystone, sandy Claystone and sandstone beds.

        Fahliyan Formation

        From 3983 to 4300 m MD (TD),

          This formation is start with light gray, light green gray, soft Limestone interbedded with Marl

         and Argillaceous Limestone and sticky Claystone beds. This formation at continue is consisting

          of medium argillaceous Limestone and medium hard Chalky Limestone beds.

        March 2016                      Page 45 of 223           Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────

       C.3. Lesson Learned and Recommendations

      26” Hole Section:

          This section generally being drilled without any major drilling problem, Bits with Center Jet

          Application (18-20/32) would reduce Bit balled up possibility.

       17 ½” Hole Section:

          Pipe stuck and tight holes during drilling of Aghajari Formation and while tripping out for

          running 13-3/8” casing running observed in 17-1/2” Hole Section of nearby wells.

        One of the main drilling challenge in 17 ½” Hole section is instability of Aghajari Formation

           in Red & Green Marl of this formation, Control Mud FL and adding KCl (as per mud program)

         by application of Polymer Mud From Below 20” casing point is highly recommended. The FL

          should be gradually lowered in Control range while combination of KCl with Long Chain

         Polymer would effect to Marl Activation properties. Time dependent behavior of Marl in this

           section should be considered and try to reduce operation time duration. Each 350 meter

          condition  trip  is required to find certainty about time dependent behavior of Aghajari

          Formation.

       12 ¼” Hole section:

         According to caliper logs, there was washout in Gachsaran Formation and should be considered

           in CMT program.

         While drilling with inclination in the formation alternated with anhydrite and marl (Hard

          formation interbedded with soft formation), the well inclination is easy to vary, so check well

           inclination by Totco more than normal in top hole.

         While drilling in massive salt formation of Gachsaran Formation, be careful of well trajectory

         and the salt’s cave-in/dissolving.

        The Gachsaran will probably contain abnormal pressured brine due to combination of

         overburden pressure compaction and tectonic forces. So, high mud weight is required to

          prevent brine flow into the well bore. This is supported by the occurrence of several flows from

          the Gachsaran Formation in some wells of the field, where the mud weight was raised to kill

          the well effectively. Calcium ion contamination should be monitored more than 3 times a day.

        March 2016                      Page 56 of 223           Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                    Nearby Wells                          Contract No.: MTN-94-854

         Well flowing in 12-1/4” hole section occurred. Adjust mud weight to prevention well flow and

          increasing in CA++ concentration.

       8 ½” Hole Section

           Erratic torque in some intervals and pipe stuck observed in 8-1/2” Hole section. Adjust mud

          weight, perform hole condition and spot pill periodically and optimize hydraulic of bit and HSI

     & JIF to solve mentioned problems.

         Well flowing, gas flowing and observe methane gas occurred in this wells in 8-1/2” Hole

           Section. Monitor well carefully and Adjust mud weight.

          Lost circulation is the main downhole problem in this part of south Azadegan field. The losses

          occurred in Pabdeh, Gurpi, Ilam and Sarvak formations. Adjust mud weight, pump LCM

           periodically.

        The drilling cutting is very difficult to move from the bottom hole to the surface due to

         improper mud properties, especially in deeper depths. Then the cutting is easy to be deposited

         and cause sticking, particularly while stopping the pump.

         Formation gas from Asmari, Pabdeh, Ilam and Sarvak formations containing harmful hydrogen

           sulfide (H2S) has the risks to harm site personnel during the operation. All site personnel should

         pay more attention on H2S hazards, and all H2S equipment should be on site and in good

           conditions.

         Glycol/polymer mud is proposed for this section to combat with mechanical/chemical bore

          hole instability of Ilam & Lafan Shale. KCl & GlyCol percentage should be keep as per mud

         program and try by maximum GPM & Adjusting Mud rheology (based on Mud Program) to

           find good cutting transport and reduce pack off type stuck risk.

       6 1/8” Hole Section

          Mud weight cut is also possible in Fahliyan formation while BTMs UP without any gain

              observation in static. That is recommended prior each trip place HI Vis at BTM to

               eliminate gas migration. Any increasing MW without confirming with Office  is not

            recommended, in some cases increasing MW would show reversed effect.

       D.1. Rig Move and Pre-spud

         Safe arrangements for rig up and rig down.

         Ensure HSE guidelines are followed.

        Hold pre-move meeting.

        Rig down the assigned rig safely at old location according to manufacturer rig down

                 procedure.

         Before move to new location, other two cellars (A & C) on the new location (Pad 44)

                 should be covered securely.

        Move all loads to new location (UTM39N: 194483; 3454673) according to HSE policy

               and road and traffic policy.

        R/up the rig safely at pad 44 location according to manufacturer rig up procedure.

        Check all equipment and tools at lower working rate to ensure that those are working

                  properly.

         All Rig equipment and  facilities should be checked based on approved pre-spud

                   checklist.

        Check and control stock and make sure that the minimum required materials are on

                   location.

            D.2.2. Sequence of 26” Hole Section Operation

         Make up 26” BHA as per BHA design. Tag formation at ±14m

           Drill ahead formation down to 151 mt (20” casing point in Aghajari formation) using hi-

                    vis bentonite pill. Pay more attention to drill straight hole.

          Note:

                      26" TD is based on 151 mt inside Aghajari formation.

                      TD should be adjusted to have 1m of rat hole below casing shoe.

                      The exact TD will be determined based on casing tally.

                      Run TOTCO inclinometer at 151 mt or each trip to evaluate the verticality of the

                            hole.

                      Ensure that all drilling tools and equipment are on well site and are in serviceable

                           condition.

                      Ensure that adequate fishing tools for this section of hole are available.

                      Enough mud material, LCM and cement should be available.

                      Mud logging unit should be available from Rig Spud.

          At TD, Circulate and clean the hole. Check the shaker to ensure hole cleaning. Drop

           TOTCO to check hole straightness. POOH to surface. Check Bit.

          Perform a condition trip including 26” reamer due to conditioning the hole prior to run

                   casing. If observe any indication of drag, run back to bottom. Wash and ream to make sure

                    that the hole condition is good.

          Perform casing running pre job safety meeting.

          R/up casing running equipment.

         Run ±151 mt (Approx. 15 Jts) 20”, 94 ppf, K-55, BTC casing with duplex shoe (Float Shoe,

                   20", K-55, 94#, BTC, stab-in type, dual valves) to bottom as per casing program. Casing

                 shoe should be adjusted to have 1 mt of rat hole and enough stick up (1.5 mt on rig floor).

                      Fill casing by non LCM fresh mud.

          Note:

                      Take care when running casing not to set down excessive weight if tight hole or

                         ledges are encountered.

                      Use 26" x 20" bow centralizer on each 3 joints, (Approx. 6 No.)

                       Visually and mechanically check the float shoe for proper operation.

                      Ensure that all casing joints have been drifted, including 20" Swage (If required to

                     wash Casing down)

          July 2016                       Page 61 of 223             Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                Sequence of Operation                     Contract No.: MTN-94-854

                      M/up 20", 94 lb/ft, K-55, Buttress, duplex shoe. If it is not already made up on joint,

                             soft make up on first joint, P/up and make up to the optimum torque. Do not engage

                          the tong close to bottom of shoe. Use thread lock compound for shoe connection.

          P/up & run stinger using the “C” plate and 5” x 18-3/4” centralizer. Sting in. M/up and test

                cementing lines. Circulate for one casing annulus volume. While circulating, check the DPs

                 annulus for any return and ensure stinger sealing. Use more DPs weight on the string if

                 observing any minor leakage through DPs annulus.

         Cement casing as per program. Any minor adjustment will be done in due time. Displace

                 with mud (2-5 bbls Short Displace). Monitor for pressures. Record pressure. Sting out.

               Check the float. POOH one stand, Circulate to flush/clean drill pipe.

          After cement returns to cellar floor, open the port on conductor & flush it.

         POOH to surface. L/D stinger.

         Keep the casing on the elevator until cement surface samples get hard. Check for casing to

                be center inside conductor pipe.

          If there is no cement return to surface, a top-up job should be performed to fill the annulus.

              Use same slurry weight as the primary cement slurry. Ensure the availability of adequate

                X-over for connecting line pipe to cement line. The cement should be accelerated with max.

               (2%) Calcium Chloride and no other additives. Wait for the top job cement to get hard.

          Check cement hardness by slack off weight.

          Note:

                      Run pilot lab test for cement slurry.

                      Take sample from pumped cement.

                      Based on the results obtained from cementing, decision will be made to perform

                          top jobs.

          Cut conductor pipe. Rough cut landing joint and L/D C.P. and L.J. Dress and bevel 20”

                    suitable to install and to weld casing head housing.

          Install and weld Casing Head Housing 20-3/4" – 3000 psi on casing stub and test the sealing

                  of welded Casing (limit pressure to a maximum of 80% of the collapse strength of the

                  casing or maximum 400 psi). Align C.H.H. outlets with Cellar stairs.

          According to cellar height (3.4 mt) which is not standard, the top of C.H.H. should not be

               more than 85 cm upper than the floor of cellar, distance between its top flange and ground

                   level must be 2.55 mt (If cellar is standard the distance between its top flange and ground

                   level must be 1.73 mt).

          July 2016                       Page 62 of 223             Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                Sequence of Operation                     Contract No.: MTN-94-854

          Note:

                      Before installing the casing head housing, open all the test ports and clean inside it

                        with water and air.

                      Check the 20-3/4” wear bushing, 20-3/4” Plug Tester and 13-3/8” Casing Slip and

                            seal assembly in C.H.H. before installing C.H.H. (on Surface).

          N/up 20-3/4" 3K + 20-3/4” 3K Spacer Spool + 20-3/4" 3K + 20-3/4” 3K Drilling Spool +

                 20-3/4" 3K Double Rams and 20-3/4” 3K Annular Preventer and related lines and valves

                and test all to 2500 psi.

          Note:

                      The height of spacer spool must be designed where the outlet of spool placed above

                          the ground level.

          N/up Bell Nipple & flow line.

          The rig should be centered again.

          Stand drill pipes enough for 2000 mt.

          Fill up the following forms and send to drilling office:

                     WPA

                      20” Casing Tally

                      20” Casing Running Report

                      20” CSG Cement Report

                      DAS (Data Acquisition System for Pumping Cement) Report

                      20” Hole section Summary

                      Wellhead Diagram with Dimension and Serial Number.

            D.2.3. Logging Program

          Logging program is not considered for this hole section.

            D.2.4. Hole Deviation

          This hole section should be drilled vertically. Taking a TOTCO survey before each POOH

                    will give a good indication of the hole profile.

            D.3.2. Sequence of 17-1/2” Hole Section Operation

         Make up 17-1/2” BHA as per BHA Design. RIH, tag top of cement. Drill out cement &

                 shoe and rat hole cement.

          Take special care for abnormal torques while drilling the Casing Shoe to minimize the risk

                  of shoe back off. Report problem immediately.

          Drill ahead formation down to 175 mt (25 mt below 20” casing shoe) with hi-vis pill.

          Circulate and clean hole, POOH to surface and P/up the same bit & BHA @ according to

           BHA program, RIH to bottom.

          Drill ahead 17-1/2” hole section down to casing point @ ±1255 mt (55 mt inside Gachsaran

                  formation), at the top of Gachsaran to case off full soft unconsolidated formation of lower

                 Aghajari and drill the next hole section with higher mud density and Provide sufficient kick

                   tolerance for drilling to next section.

          Note:

                      Adjust mud weight according to program and as hole condition dictate.

                      Drop Totco on each bit change trip (or each 300 mt as per request) at depth lower

                         than 1000 mt and for deeper depth run Totco by sandline.

                      TD should be adjusted in such a way to have 1 meters of rat hole for probable

                               fillings, before running casing.

                       Section TD maybe changed based on formation tops detected by wellsite geologist.

                      Do not forget to perform SLM pipes while POOH.

                      Choke manifold and choke line must be lined up for SOFT SHUT-IN through pit

                          or burning line.

                       Test BOP’s and related equipment as per NIOC policy each 14 Days.

                      Ensure that adequate fishing tools for this section are available.

                      Ensure that enough amount and type of LCM are available and keep ready to mix

                       and pump LCM Pills to cure losses.

                      Before each condition trip and when the ROP become low, clean the hole with

                            circulating Salt water.

          At section TD, circulate, clean hole. Perform wiper trip to shoe and observe well @ shoe.

               RIH, Check for any fill. CBU on bottom. Spot 100 bbls hi-vis pill @ bottom.

         POOH to surface, if observed any indication of tight hole, run back to TD. W&R to make

                  sure that the hole condition is good prior to run casing.

          Retrieve Wear Bushing. Perform pre-job safety meeting.

         Run CDR/GR/CALIPER Log.

          July 2016                       Page 65 of 223             Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                Sequence of Operation                     Contract No.: MTN-94-854

         Run Sonic Log.

          Perform condition trip before run casing.

          Change top rams to 13-3/8”.

         R/U casing running equipment. Perform pre-job safety meeting.

         Run casing as per casing program. Run ±1255 mt (Approx. 111 Jts) 13-3/8", 68 ppf, N-80,

            BTC casing.

          Casing shoe depth should be adjusted in such a way to have 1 meters of rat hole and enough

                    stick up (1.5 m) on rig floor.

          Note:

                      Ensure that the all casing is drifted; cleaned, tallied and spaced out priors to RIH.

                      Perform Float Shoe and Float Collar Function test.

                       Fill casing by non LCM fresh mud every 10 joints.

                      Take care when running casing not to set down excessive weight if tight hole are

                         encountered.

                       13-3/8”, 68 lb/ft, N-80, BTC Float Collar should be installed two joint above the

                             float shoe.

                      Pump complete casing volume @ shoe and on TD.

                      Use 17-1/2" x 13-3/8" bow centralizer on each five joints (34 No.)

                       13-3/8” x 20” Rigid Centralizers on third and fourth joints from top.

                      Use thread locking compound for Shoe track.

                       Precautionary wash the last joint.

         When casing is at casing setting depth, circulate while preparing for cementing M/up & test

                cementing lines. Circulate for 120% of casing annulus volume.

         M/up cementing head. Load the plug. WSS shall witness the loading. Test the lines to 3000

                   psi for 10 minutes.

         Cement casing as per program. Any minor adjustment will be done in due time. Displace

                 with mud. Monitor pressures. Bump plug and perform casing integrity test with 1500 psi

                    for 15 min.

          Bleed off pressure. Check for back flow (If any back flow was observed, hold the pressure

                   inside the Casing and let the cement to get hard).

          Note:

                      Run pilot lab test for cement slurry.

                      Take sample from pumped cement.

          July 2016                       Page 66 of 223             Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                Sequence of Operation                     Contract No.: MTN-94-854

                      Based on the results obtained from cementing, decision will be made to perform

                         top-up jobs.

          Hold on casing string weight. Drop 20-3/4” x 13-3/8” automatic casing hanger. Slack off

            80% of casing string weight. Test hanger to 800 - 1000 psi pressure. (Open base flange side

                    outlet valves while testing).

         N/down BOP. Rough cut L.J. Dress and bevel casing neck.

          Install Casing Head Spool 20-3/4”-3K x 13-5/8”-5K. Pack & Test p-seals & Cavity (75%

            CSG Collapse).

          Note:

                      Before installing the casing head spool, open all the test ports and clean inside it

                        with water and air.

            D.4.2. Sequence of 12-1/4” Hole Section Operation

         Make up 12-1/4” BHA as per BHA design include Junk Sub. RIH. Tag top of cement (Top

                   Plug). Drill out cement to 7 mt top of shoe.

                       If During Cementing Operation, Bumping did not observe during cementing,

                       perform CIT w/1500 psi for 15 min.

          Displace new mud according to mud program.

          Continue drill out Plugs, Float Collar, Shoe Track, shoe and rat hole to 3-5 mt below shoe

                     at new formation. Clean out the rat hole, circulate clean. Work on junk sub. Watch carefully

                    for any indication of abnormal torque, since there might be the risk of shoe back off.

          Perform FIT at new formation (EMW should be equal to max. planned mud weight for 12-

                  1/4” hole section plus 2 PCF, Approx. 112 PCF based on mud program).

          Continue drilling Formation to 25 mt below shoe (±1280 mt).

          Circulate and clean hole. Work on junk sub. POOH to surface. L/down junk sub.

          P/up new bit & string stabilizer @ 20 mt, RIH to bottom.

          Continue drilling formation to Casing Point @ ±1555 mt at the bottom of Gachsaran (5 mt

                above Asmari) to isolate Gachsaran high pressure salt water formation in order to open the

                    reservoir. Help prevent sloughing and enlargement of hole during deeper drilling and

                 Provide sufficient kick tolerance for drilling to next section.

          Note:

                       Test BOP’s and related equipment as per NIOC policy each 14 Days.

                      Choke line and choke manifold must be lined up for SOFT SHUT-IN.

                      Adjust mud weight according to nearby wells and as hole condition dictation.

                      The Section TD maybe changed based on formation tops detected by the well site

                           geologist.

                      TD should be adjusted in such a way to have 1 meters of rat hole for probable

                               fillings, before running casing.

                      The mud engineer shall check the mud properties as pre-planned time schedule

                           especially for (Ca++, THD, Chloride, pH, F.L, Gels, and Mud weight).

                      Ensure that all drilling tools and equipment are on well site in serviceable condition.

                      Ensure that adequate fishing tools are available for this section.

                      Ensure that enough amounts of LCM are available at location.

                      Do not forget to perform SLM pipes while POOH.

          At section TD, circulate, clean hole. Perform wiper trip to shoe and observe well @ shoe.

               RIH, Check for any fill. CBU on bottom. Spot 100 bbls hi-vis pill @ bottom.

          July 2016                       Page 70 of 223             Drilling Engineering Department

         POOH to surface. If observed any indication of drag, run back to bottom. Wash and ream

                    to make sure that the hole condition is good.

          Retrieve Wear Bushing. Perform pre-job safety meeting.

         Run CDR/GR/CALIPER Log.

         Run DSI Log.

          Perform condition trip before run casing.

          Change top rams to 9-5/8”.

          R/up casing running equipment. Perform pre-job safety meeting.

         Run casing as per casing program. Run ±1555 mt (Approx. 137 Jts) 9-5/8", 53.5 ppf, T-95,

                  Especial Drift, HSM3 casing.

          Casing shoe depth should be adjusted in such a way to have 1 meters of rat hole and enough

                    stick up (1.5 m) on rig floor.

          Note:

                      Ensure that the all casing is drifted; cleaned, tallied and spaced out priors to RIH.

                      Perform Float Shoe and Float Collar Function test.

                       Fill casing by non LCM fresh mud every 10 joints.

                      Take care when running casing not to set down excessive weight if tight hole are

                         encountered.

                       9-5/8”, 53.5 lb/ft, T-95, Especial Drift, HSM3 Float Collar should be installed two

                             joint above the float shoe.

                      Pump a complete casing volume @ shoe and on TD.

                      Use 12-1/4" x 9-5/8" bow centralizer on each five joints (30 No.)

                       9-5/8” x 13-3/8” Rigid Centralizers on third and fourth joints from top.

                      Use thread locking compound for Shoe track.

                       Precautionary wash the last joint.

         M/up cementing head. Load the plug. WSS shall witness the loading. Test the lines to 3000

                   psi for 10 minutes. Make sure that the cement head is capable of holding pressure while

                   displacing and testing cement (Visually).

          Circulate 1.2 times of casing volume with low GPM up to clean the shaker.

          Perform cementing as per program. Any minor adjustment of cement volume in regards to

                   correction of depth will be done in due time. Displace with mud. Monitor for pressures.

                Record and report bumping pressure (Bump plug and test casing with 3000 psi for 15min).

                Bleed off pressure. Check for back flow. If any back flow observed, hold the pressure inside

                  casing until the cement is hard.

                      Drop ball and M/up combination plug dropping head to the last joint. Use pup joint,

            D.6.2. Sequence of 5-7/8” Hole Section Operation

         Make up 5-7/8” BHA as per BHA design include Junk Sub, RIH to top of CMT (Top Plug).

          Drill out plugs, float collar, cement to 7 mt top of shoe.

                       If During Cementing Operation, Bumping did not observe during cementing,

                       perform CIT W/3000 psi for 15 min.

          Displace new mud according to mud program.

          Continue drill out Float shoe and rat hole to 3-5 mt below shoe at new formation. Clean out

                   the rat hole, circulate clean. Work on junk sub. Watch carefully for any indication of

                abnormal torque, since there might be the risk of shoe back off.

          Perform FIT at new formation (EMW should be equal to max. planned mud weight for 5-

                  7/8” hole section plus 2 PCF, Approx. 112 PCF based on mud program).

          Continue drilling Formation to 25 mt below shoe (±3982mt).

          Circulate and clean hole. Work on junk sub. POOH to surface. L/down junk sub.

         Run SBT-RBT/CCL/GR log through 7” Liner to evaluate cement behind Casing.

          P/up new bit & string stabilizer @ 20 mt, RIH to bottom.

         Resume drilling formation to 5” Liner Point @ ±4300 mt (inside Fahliyan Formation).

          Note:

                      Due to possibility of losses, 5-7/8" B.Bit, enough mud material & LCM should be

                           available in well site.

                      Due to high H2S Amount in this field H2S services should be available on the rig

                                 site.

                       Test BOP’s and related equipment as per NIOC policy each 7 Days.

                      Adjust mud weight according to nearby wells and as hole condition dictation.

                      The Section TD maybe changed based on formation tops detected by the well site

                           geologist.

                      TD should be adjusted in such a way to have 1 meters of rat hole for probable

                               fillings, before running liner.

                       Material balance to determine the theoretical concentrations of additives in the mud

                        should be calculated and reported to WSS. Significant deviations from program

                        should be explained.

                      Ensure that all drilling tools and equipment are on well site in serviceable condition.

                      Ensure that adequate fishing tools are available for this section.

                      Do not forget to perform SLM pipes while POOH.

                      Do not forget to take Slow Pump each 12 hours.

          July 2016                       Page 81 of 223             Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                Sequence of Operation                     Contract No.: MTN-94-854

          At section TD, circulate, clean hole. Perform wiper trip to shoe and observe well @ shoe.

               RIH, Check for any fill. CBU on bottom. Spot 60 bbls Polymer Hi-Vis. Pill @ bottom. Use

                 5-10 lbs/bbl LCM (shell fish) if necessary.

         POOH to surface. If observed any indication of drag, run back to bottom. Wash and Ream

                    to make sure that the hole condition is good.

          Retrieve Wear Bushing. Perform pre-job safety meeting.

         Run Full Suite/CDR Logs (Based on well condition, TLC method shall be applied).

         Run DSI log (Based on well condition, TLC method shall be applied).

         RIH with 5-7/8” BHA to bottom, Circ Btm's up, Condition hole prior to logging, POOH to

                   surface.

         Run MDT Log (Based on well condition, TLC method shall be applied).

         RIH with 5-7/8” BHA to bottom, Circ Btm's up, Condition hole prior to run 5” liner, POOH

                    to surface.

         RIH with 7” scraper BHA to bottom, Circ Btm's up, Scrap 5” top PKR setting depth, POOH

                    to surface.

          R/up liner running equipment.

         Run & hang (With Top Packer) 5” liner as per liner program. Run ±412 mt (Approx. 40

                      Jts) 5", 18 ppf, T-95, HSM3 liner (liner lap: ±3887 mt, liner Shoe: ±4299 mt).

          Liner shoe depth should be adjusted in such a way to have 1 meters of rat hole.

          Note:

                      Ensure that the all Liners is drifted; cleaned, tallied and spaced out priors to RIH.

                      Check Wiper Plug and Pump Down Plug with LDC for any leakage.

                       Fill Liner by non LCM fresh mud every 10 joints.

                       5”, 18 lb/ft, T-95, HSM3 Float Collar should be installed two joint above the float

                          shoe.

                      Use 5-7/8" x 7" bow centralizer on each 3 joints (20 No.)

                      5” x 7” Rigid Centralizers on third and fourth joints from top.

                      Use thread locking compound for Shoe track.

                      5” Hyd. Liner Hanger must be suitable for inside 7” CSG 35 ppf.

                      Do not forget to remove wear bushing before running log & liner.

          5” Liner running procedure:

                       After making up the shoe track, lower through rotary table and check that the floats

                          are holding. Fill up with mud and pick up to check float functionality. Circulate for

                     10 min.

          July 2016                       Page 82 of 223             Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                Sequence of Operation                     Contract No.: MTN-94-854

                      Run liner in at controlled speed to minimize pressure surges (approx. 12 joints/hr).

                     Check returns constantly.

                       Fill up liner completely every 10 joints run.

                      When the required numbers of liner joints have been made up, make up the liner

                        hanger with Top Packer, running tool as per liner hanger engineer’s instructions.

                      Run the liner with 5” and 3-1/2” DP to 7” Liner, Mud Circulation to check String,

                         Liner hanger engineer should be on rig floor while running liner into open hole.

                         NOTE :
                  Check the loss while RIH if need Decrease the Speed of the RIH. SLM &

                                         drift the drill pipe while RIH the liner.

                      Continue running Liner to Btm. Tag Btm. 1 mt move up string. Circulate mud as

                  150% of string capacity.

                      Drop ball and M/up combination plug dropping head to the last joint. Use pup joint,

                                   if necessary.

                      The Hanger will be set about ±70 mt above 7" Shoe.

                      Try to avoid setting Hanger on Casing coupling. Pressurize inside Liner and Set the

                      Hanger as per Hanger engineer procedure and Shear LDC and circulate mud.

                        Release Setting tool and ensure that the setting tool is free of the Liner Hanger

                       (work string 0.5 mt up and down – record loss of weight as the hanger sets) before

                               start Cement job.

                      Cement 5" Liner as per cementing program.

                       Displace the cement with the cementing pump to the Liner Wiper Plug. Shear Liner

                      Wiper Plug (Record  the volume pumped and  shearing  pressure). Displace

                      combined Wiper Plugs to Latch down Collar.

                      Bump the Plug with 500 psi over final displacement pressure and 1500 psi for

                     bumping pressure. (Record and report final displacement pressure prior to bump

                          the Plug). If there is no bump, do not displace more than 25% of the shoe-track

                       volume. Displace with Cementing Pump and record cement losses.

                      Hold bumping pressure for 5 minutes (If the Plugs bumped).

                       If plugs bumped, pressure up and test the liner to 3000 psi for 15 minutes.

                      Bleed off pressure slowly and check for back-flow.

                       Pull out setting tool for two meters, check for U tube F/DPs. Set Packer & N/down

                      Cementing Head.

                      POOH to top of CMT slurry estimated depth and flush string.

          July 2016                       Page 83 of 223             Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0                Sequence of Operation                     Contract No.: MTN-94-854

         Cement Liner as per Program and bumping with 3000 psi while perform casing integrity

                      test for 15 minutes. (While Mud Engineer should have level of mud Tanks and announce

                any Loss or flow).

          Pull out Setting Tools. Set Top Packer. POOH 3-5 mt. Circ as 1.5 times annulus volume.

           POOH to surface while WOC.

         RIH with 5-7/8" R.Bit to 5" Liner Lap while D/Out Cement of above Liner Lap. Clean the

                    hole. Perform Pressure test to 2000 psi. POOH to surface while L/Down last BHA and

                 excess 5" DPs.

          Note:

                      Run pilot lab test for cement slurry.

                      Take sample from pumped cement.

                       If cement of above 5" L.Lap was soft, WOC to get hard, then D/Out cement.

         RIH with 4-1/8" Mill and new BHA to 5" LDC while D/Out P.O.Bushing and cement above

             LDC. Clean the hole. Stand back 2-7/8” Drill pipes while tripping in.

                       If During Cementing Operation, Bumping was not observed, perform CIT with

                     3000 psi for 15 minutes.

         Run 7” Scraper and scrap this interval. POOH to surface.

         RIH w/7" DST tools, Perform 5" L.Lap Dry test with 2000 psi differential pressure with

                 Fahliyan pressure datum (Dry test program will be issued in due time).

         Run SBT-RBT/CCL/GR log through 5” liner to evaluate cement behind liner.

         Run VSP log from TD to Surface.

            D.6.3. Testing

             D.6.3.1. Fahliyan (F5) DST

         RIH with 4-1/8" Mill + 5" scraper. Scrape 5" liner (DST packer setting depth) (All Fahliyan

                  Test Intervals). POOH.

         RIH with TCP + APR assembly and pressure test string.

          Depth correction (Gamma ray correlation), Rig up the flowhead, Set the packer and test

                 same.

          Shoot and perforate 5" liner as per perforation program (Accurate Perforation program will

                be issued in due time).

          Perform FBDST as per program (Accurate Testing program will be issued in due time).

         Use CTU to pump water, Diesel or N2 and perform acidizing.

            D.6.4. Completion

         RIH with 5-7/8" J. Mill (M. Mill) to top of first cement plug, drill out all plugs and bridge

                  plugs to 5" liner lap, circulate bottom's up, POOH to surface.

         RIH with 5" Polish and Dress mill to top of 5" liner lap, circulate bottom's up, RIH to 5"

                       tie back receptacle, polish and clean inside the same, POOH to surface.

         RIH with 5" liner tie back assembly to liner lap (±3887 mt) and tie back liner 50 m above

                 Tarbur formation perforations (±2318 mt) and cement the same. POOH to surface and lay

              down setting tools.

         RIH with 5-7/8" R.Bit and Clean well to new 5" liner lap (approx. = ±2318 mt), POOH to

                   surface.

         RIH with 4-1/8" mill and Drill out P.O.Bushing and clean out inside 5" Liner, drill out

                cement plug and bridge plug to LDC. RIH to TD and Check TD. POOH to liner lap, perform

                     liner lap pressure test w/2000 psi, POOH to surface.

         RIH with 5-7/8" R.Bit + 7" scraper, Scrape 7" Liner (both Dry Test and completion packer

                    setting depth), POOH to surface.

         RIH with Dry Test tools and adequate water cushion to and perform dry test from liner lap.

           POOH to surface and L/Down Dry Test tools.

         Run Completion string, Set the packer and test the same (Accurate completion string and

                program will be issued in due time).

         N/Down BOP, N/Up well head and test. Complete and secure the well.

          Fill up the following forms and send them to the drilling office:

                      5” Liner Tally.

                      5” Liner Running Report.

        F.1. Drilling Fluids Program Discussion

             F.1.1. 26” Hole Size

                                 Casing
          Hole    Casing Size                     Geological
                                   Setting Depth                      Lithology     Drilling Fluids Design

         26 “       20”         151
                                              Formation        Shale         Hi-Vis Pill Gel

              F.1.1.1. Drilling Fluids Formulation

             This  is a large diameter hole and the hole cuttings removal mechanism will be a

             combination of high annular velocities and hi-viscous sweeps.

             Bentonite Mud  / Hi-Vis Pill has been planned to overcome the drilling problems and

              increase the rate of penetration. Rheological properties of the system will be increased and

             kept in the optimum range by adding BENTO-GEL API at the concentration of 20 – 30

                lb/bbl.

              Soft dispersive clays and unconsolidated sands can be expected whilst drilling this interval.

           The formation is mainly composed of unconsolidated sands with some mudstone.

    Mud Type                               Bentonite Mud/Hi-Vis Pill Gel
   MW (pcf)                                       65-68

                                                    Active Mud <100
      Funnel Viscosity (sec/qt)
                                                    Hi-Vis Pill = 110

     PV (cP)                                  7 – 10 ( ALAP )

       Fluid Loss (cc/30 min)                    NC
     pH                                                 9.5 – 10.5

                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0            Mud & Cement Program                    Contract No.: MTN-94-854

               Mixing Procedure
            1. Fill the tanks with the required volume of Drill water.

                        a.  Note: Drill water should be tested before mixing and if its hardness is more than

                      400mg/l, it should be treated.

            2. Add 0.5-1 lb/bbl of CAUSTIC SODA to prepare brine to pH 10-10.5.

            3. Add 0.7-1 lb/bbl SODA ASH to treat out any hardness greater than 400 mg/l.

            4. Add 20-30 lb/bbl of Bentonite and allow hydrating for as long as possible.

            5. Add 1 lb/bbl LIME to system to adjust pH

              F.1.1.2. Interval Key Issues

                  Loss Zone: There is a possibility of complete loss in this interval with No Return

                mud, so LCM pill should be prepared before drilling of this section as contingency.

                   Hole Cleaning: Hole cleaning problems could be avoided by pumping hi-
                     viscous pill more frequently and of greater volume.

               F.1.1.3. Engineering Outline & System Maintenance

                   To achieve better Ca++ settling, pH have to be kept above 10.

                   The Plastic Viscosity value should be minimized by optimum usage of the solids
                      control equipment and by dilution with pre-mix where applicable, to generate new
                  volume and / or reduce mud rheology.

                   Yield point should be kept high (the program range) to reduce the annular flow
                        rates and gel value required to reach to maximum hole cleaning.

                    It is essential to remove drilled solids rather than dilute with water to acceptable
                         levels. Therefore every effort must be made to maximize the efficiency of the
                        solids control equipment.

                   Due to the hole size, and the likely pump rates available, a 6 rpm in excess of 12
                            is advised. In order to achieve this, a quantity of pre-hydrated BENTO-GEL API
                        will be needed in the system make up.

        March 2016                       Page 98 of 223          Drilling Engineering Department

               F.1.1.4. Engineering Outline & System Maintenance

         These recommendation were prepared based on lesson learned of previous drilled wells, they
         can be useful to drill this well if used.

                   Appropriate GPM is required due to large hole diameter to provide good hole
                       cleaning.

                   Formation losses has been recorded in nearby well, so enough amount of LCMs
                     should be available while drilling.

              F.1.2.1. Drilling Fluids Properties

        To solve problems associated with this interval and increase the ROP, Saturated KCL

        Polymer Mud (CLAY-BOND FLUID SYSTEM) has been designed to drill the formation

         and inhibit clay and marl problems in this section.

       CLAY-BOND L has also been successfully used to drill clay interbeds in both saline and non-

           saline systems. It is very important to determine MBT of mud during drilling and keep it

          in the program range. Controlling of MW could be done by running SCE and dressing the

          shakers with finest mesh screens, start with 67 pcf MW and increase every 90 meter 1 pcf using

            salts and required BARA-WATE API up to 79 pcf.

   Mud Type                               Saturated KCL Polymer Mud (CLAY-BOND FLUID SYSTEM)

                                                   Start with 67 pcf MW, increase every 90 meter 1 pcf
  MW (pcf)
                                                       up to 79 pcf.

         Mixing Procedure
              1-  Fill the tanks with 100 bbl of Drill water.
              2- Add 65-70 lb/bbl of SODIUM CHLORIDE AC (NaCl).
              3- Add 0.5-1 lb/bbl of CAUSTIC SODA to adjust pH around 10-11.
              4- Add 2%vol ROP ENHANCER (2 Drum/100 bbl)
              5- When ROP is low pump this pill or pump 50bbl of this pill every 100m.

              F.1.2.4. Interval Key Issues

             Loss Zone: There is a possibility of complete loss in this interval in Pabdeh Formation

                 with No Return mud, so LCM pill should be prepared before drilling this section as

                  contingency.

              Bit Balling: Due to the clay and marl characteristic of the formation, bit balling is one

                  of the main problems. It is required to keep the KCL R-UPG percent between 2 – 5 %

                and use CLAY-BOND L to provide cutting encapsulation during drilling.

             Clay & Marl Inhibition: Tight hole might be experienced in this interval due to

                   existence of clay & marl in the formation. Good inhibition will be helpful to provide

                    stable hole condition.

              Salt Water Flow: There is a possibility of salt water flow. Therefore close monitoring

                        is required to maintain Ca++ and pH level.

              F.1.2.5. Engineering Outline & System Maintenance

            Mud weight to be kept between 67 and 75 pcf unless hole dictates, salts and BARA-

           WATE API will be used to rise the mud weight. Controlling of MW could be done by

                 running SCE and dressing the shakers with finest mesh screens (150/ 200 mesh size)

                         if possible.

            Dump and dilution is one of the best ways to keep the MW on the guideline especially

                 with clean mud systems. Running all solids equipment and dump and dilution

               would be the best way to control the MW (after drilling each stand , 25 bbl can be

              dumped from sandtrap).

             Rheological properties of the mud should be monitored to ensure maximum hole

                   cleaning.

        March 2016                       Page 105 of 223         Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0            Mud & Cement Program                    Contract No.: MTN-94-854

             Contamination with cement will increase the rheological properties of the mud especially

                   the yield point. Therefore, prior to cement, YP should be reduced to the minimum level

                         if practical.

         F.1.2.6. Lesson Learned and Recommendation

         These recommendation were prepared based on lesson learned of previous drilled wells, they

         can be useful to drill this well if used.

               It is very important to determine MBT of mud during drilling and keep it in the program

                   range.

             Running all solids equipment and dump and dilution would be the best way to control

                   the MW.

             Due to undesirable hole condition and hard casing running, regular wash and ream should

                 be performed on each drilled stand.

              Increasing mud weight smoothly, improve hole condition in previous well.

             Regarding high ROP, pre-treatment is a critical item while drilling this section.

             Based on the reports of wells 84 & 93 (nearby wells of APP4), 17.5” hole section of these

                  wells were drilled with Bentonite mud system, at the end of this section while POOH

          & RIH tight hole occurred. Improvement  in hole conditions can be seen with

                  optimization of fluid properties and the use of appropriate inhibitive additives and fluid

                 systems such as CLAY BOND SYSTEM.

             Due to in the previous wells ,bit balling was occurred so Hi Vis Pill (periodically) and

                    Salt Water (optional) should be pumped in the hole for hole cleaning also TORQUE

             FREE GB M600 & TORQUE FREE GB M300 should be added to system.

              F.1.3.1. Drilling Fluids Formulation

        To drill this section a Salt Saturated Polymer Mud with 115-125 pcf would be used. Salt would

         be used up to the saturation level (75 pcf) to prohibit the thin inter bedded layers of Salt in

        member 7 & 2 from being washed and also will act as a strong shale inhibitor in the system

         and maintain MW as per pore pressure curve dictates in the drilling program using BARA-

      WATE API.

       Mud Weight should be increased 1 pcf every 02m up to 105 pcf, more than 105 pcf should be

         done with company man permission.

         Rheology of the system would be raised by using BIO-XCD (As Optional).

      AMYLOSE LV, a modified starch would be used as a filtration control agent in this section.

        The main concern in this section is the effect of the formation water on the mud properties. If

         any problem rises during the drilling, AMYLOSE LV should be replaced with the PAC ULV

            at the concentration between 1-1.5 lb/bbl.

              F.1.3.2. Interval Key Issues

                  Salt Water Flow: Gachsaran formation has the potential of salt water flow. The

                    hardness of this water is between 35000-53000 mg/l, so any increase in the hardness

                     of mud would be an indication of possible salt water flow. Therefore, during drilling

                         this section, hardness of the system must be checked and monitored three times per

                  day for any increase in Ca++ ions.

                  Losses: In the upper part of Gachsaran a weak zone has been experienced. Layers

                     interbedded in the salts in Gachsaran Members 2 & 3 can have normal pressure, and

                      therefore a reduced fracture gradient compared to the salt layers. Heavy losses

                   might be experienced in Gachsaran.

              Losses have also been seen in the first shale layer of Cap Rock, its second layer from the

                 top, so it is very important not to drill deeper than approximately one meter into this

             Member.

                  Stuck Pipe: Tight hole / stuck pipe in the 12 1/4" hole section is typically isolated

                       to the Shales/Clay stones and salt beds. The stuck in Shales and clay stones as far

                     as the MW is adjusted correctly and FL is tight and the salinity is up to saturation

                           is not a matter of concern.

        March 2016                       Page 109 of 223         Drilling Engineering Department

────────────────────────────────────────────
                    South Azadegan

                  APP-04 Drilling Program

Doc No.: NDCAZD-EN-WP-004-0            Mud & Cement Program                    Contract No.: MTN-94-854

              F.1.3.3. Engineering Outline & System Maintenance

          Mud Weight
                In the initial mixing, salt would be the first weighting agent up to 75 pcf, then BARA

         WATE API will be used to reach to 105 pcf as hole conditions dictate. Controlling the

           mud weight meanwhile drilling would be done by running all SCE and dump and dilution.

              Pore pressure is expected to be marginally above the normal pressure in the Jurassic /

             Upper Triassic.

          Mud weight selection is critical in this interval. In some offset wells, excessive losses

           may occur in the Gachsaran member 7 when the mud weight is higher than the optimum.

           On the other hand, in some offset wells, water flow could be encountered when the mud

               weight is allowed to fall down below optimum MW.

                     It is important to drill this section with the mud weight being kept as per the guide line,

                also by running all solids control equipments and dressing the shakers with finest mesh

                screens; it is possible to minimize the ECD and the potential for induced losses.

           Shale Inhibition
                 Salt would be used for shale inhibition to combat the Gachsaran Marl/Shale’s. However,

                the very tight FL would be the other shale inhibition property of the system. Controlling

                the salinity up to saturation is vital to drill into Gachsaran formation against troublesome

                Shales/Marls.

           Rheological Properties
          PV is always to be kept as low as possible by reducing the drilled solids concentrations

               of mud using the proper installation of the solids control equipment so that the hydraulics

                    is optimized.

             High gel values are required to support the high quantity of weighting agents while

                 tripping, making connections and during survey times. PV should be kept in the range

              mentioned in the drilling fluid program.

         A thin salt bed in Gachsaran member 2 is a red flag for Geologist that means reaching to

              cap rock is imminent, salinity below the saturation will cause the salt samples to be

                dissolved in the system resulting in no salt sample over the shakers.

                F.1.3.4. Lesson Learned and Recommendation
         These recommendation were prepared based on lesson learned of previous drilled wells, they

         can be useful to drill this well if used.

               Due to undesirable hole condition while tripping and casing running, performing wash

               and ream on each drilled stand is the best way to have good hole condition.

                Availability and running decenter centrifuge to remove LGS in this section is critical.

                It is important to drill this section with the mud weight being kept as per the guide line.

                Also by running all solids control equipment and dressing the shakers with finest mesh

                  screens; it is possible to minimize the ECD and the potential for induced losses.

              A low as PV is required in this section to eliminate Mud Gel Up by suitable SCE layout.

               Based on the reports of wells 93(nearby well of APP4) in this section flow observed

                   that changed the mud properties such as increasing hardness and Ca++, in order to

                 prevent flow, at the start drilling of this section MW should be adjusted 90 pcf and

                 increased it every 20 m 1 pcf up to 105 pcf at the end of section.
